DAEMON ROOT / PROVIDERS — PRE-EDIT DISPOSITION RECORD
Scope: every direct-root *.test.ts / *.check.ts plus providers/*.test.ts. No daemon launch or live socket operations.
Method: current test and production owners read; package scripts checked. Regex/AST inventory is indexing only.
Validation per named file: pnpm exec node --import tsx --import ./scripts/test/assert-ok.mjs --import ./scripts/test/quiet-mock-timers.mjs --test --test-concurrency=2 <path>. Standalone checks run without --test.
Risk for KEEP/REWRITE: removing the case would lose the concrete failure named below; tests use scratch storage/processes. DELETE risks/stronger coverage are explicit.
For each retained case the six numbered clauses below apply WITH its specific failure and oracle; no claim that exports or internal representations themselves are a contract.

Test changes: 3 DELETE, 8 REWRITE, 423 KEEP entries in original 434-entry inventory.
No production behavior changed. No support file or production seam became unused: Git fake runner remains required for independent process errors, repository serialization and safety; all provider fixtures retain direct consumers.
Net test diff: 77 inserted / 83 removed lines.

Renamed rewrites (the disposition entries above retain original names):
- branches derive ahead/behind from %(upstream:track) -> branches report divergence from the upstream and omit remote HEAD aliases
- commitDetail parses -z name-status/numstat rename records (old-then-new) -> commitDetail reports Unicode renames and binary changes
- stash list splits on the record separator and unwraps git's WIP subject -> stash list returns newest first with branch and named or default message

Validation: the five-file full test command started before edits passed 113/113; its processes loaded files over time, so final changed scenarios were verified separately, as recorded below.
Final own-file git diff --check: passed.
